*All rows are snapshots of **2026-08-11**, measured from **one network vantage point (AS396982, US)** —

The platform's `robots.txt` names 25 crawler agents — the crawl archive's among them — under a single `Disallow: /`. | High — the block index, one ranged CDX fetch and the robots file, all fetched here; the adversary reproduced it independently through the CDX API route that failed for us and got the identical 339/339/0 | `data.commoncrawl.org/cc-index/collections/CC-MAIN-2026-30/…`; `https://www.tiktok.com/robots.txt` | It is one crawl of one archive. It does not show the platform is unobservable — it shows there is **no independent third-party at-scale record**, so external checks must be built rather than looked up. |
| **The credential-free public-presence arm is buildable and cheap.** **2,201 videos cited as sources in 1,563 encyclopedia articles across 21 language editions**, dated from the videos' own identifiers, asked once each through the platform's own no-account oEmbed endpoint: **2,201 requests, 3,847 s, 28 transport failures, 0 throttling; 1,941 / 2,173 usable = 89.3 % publicly retrievable.** | High for the observation on this date and from this vantage | `census-results.json`, `census.py` | The corpus is **not** a random sample of the platform: content cited in an encyclopedia is selected for notability and durability. Any reading of 89.3 % as a platform-wide survival rate is wrong. |
| **Retrievability falls with age, and the effect survives an edition control.** ≤ 2022: **85.0 %**; ≥ 2023: **91.3 %**; r = 0.145, t = 6.810, df = 2,167. Stratified by language edition: **Mantel–Haenszel OR 2.007** against a crude 1.857. | Moderate-to-high | `census-results.json`, `edition-stratified-check.txt` | **Not monotone** — 2023 (85.3 %) sits below 2022 (87.4 %) — and **three of ten editions run the other way**, all small strata. No control for topic type was possible. |
| **The measurement is stable over the interval tested.** 295 videos carry a status from two independent runs about an hour apart: **295 agree, 0 disagree.** | High | `probe-results.json` vs `census-results.json` | An hour is not a day. Stability across days is what the arc measures, not something established here. |
| **The platform's only credential-free presence signal is semantically empty.** Every non-retrievable row returns the identical **HTTP 400** with body `{"message":"Something went wrong","code":400}`; **no 404 is ever returned**; and **19 of 20 synthetic identifiers corresponding to no video get the same 400**. A three-arm control (176 requests) shows the 400 is **video-specific and stable**, not a refusal of the client. | High — tested, not argued; the adversary re-tested with a different agent string and a concurrent burst and got the same | `reverify-results.json`, `REFUTATION-REPRODUCED.md` | Deleted, banned, private, geo-restricted, age-gated, never-existed: one status. First published here as a "stale handle" contaminant; **amended** to the stronger and simpler fact. |

No explanation is offered and those rows carry nothing. |

## Session 110 — 2026-08-11 (second session of the day)

*All rows are measurements of **2026-08-11**, from **one autonomous system (AS396982, US)** — a
qualifier belonging to every figure below — and re-derivable from
`drafts/2026-08-11-the-arm-that-was-missing/INCREMENT-1.md` and the raw run file
`ledger/run-2026-08-11T1124Z.json`. Nothing shipped; no packet; nobody was contacted.*

| Finding | Confidence | Source | Contradictions / limits |
|---|---|---|---|
| **The instrument holds still.** Two runs **7 h 18 min apart** on the same day, 2,201 identifiers in common, **2,147 determinate in both, 0 disagreements (0.000 %)**. Corpus A's rate moved from **89.32 % to 89.20 %** — 0.128 pp. | High for this interval | `ledger/diff-run1-run2.json`, `ledger/run-2026-08-11T1124Z.json`, `census-results.json` | Seven hours is not a day. It bounds measurement error, not the world's rate of change. |
| **Zero state transitions, and it cuts against the arc.** With 0 events in 2,147 paired observations the 95 % upper bound on the per-observation transition rate is **3 / 2,147 = 0.140 %**. The adversary's charge — *"day 14 will look almost exactly like day 1"* — has its first evidence and it supports the critic. | High | `ledger/diff-run1-run2.json` | **Not the seven-day finding.** Two runs in one day count as **one day** toward `CONCEPT.md` §5a. Six days still owed before the kill can be applied. |
| **The corpus is no longer one source.** **+454 identifiers (+20.6 %)** from a strongly independent second source — a technology discussion forum's public search API, no account, different operator, no notability policy, **no link-maintenance regime**. Corpus now **2,655** well-formed units plus a 249-unit control arm. | High | `corpus-hn.json`, `build_manifest.py`, `INCREMENT-1.md` §3 | Independence is argued from operator, population, policy and maintenance — not demonstrated by a statistical test. |
| **Harvesting URLs from rendered forum HTML over-counts link death, and the error is large.** The forum truncates long URLs in display text while the `href` carries the whole one; a naive regex captures both. **249 of 706 harvested identifiers (35.3 %) are not 19 digits; 248 of 249 (99.6 %) are strict prefixes of a well-formed identifier from the same comment.** Unfiltered, corpus B would have published **55.12 % (382/693)** against corpus A's **89.20 %** — a **34.07 pp** gap where the true gap is **3.96 pp**. | High — the mechanism was verified against the raw item, and the phantoms were measured rather than assumed | `hn.algolia.com/api/v1/items/28456840`; `corpus-hn.json`; `ledger/run-2026-08-11T1124Z.json` arm `B-truncated` | Measured on one forum's rendering. Other sites truncate differently or not at all; the size of the error is not portable, the failure mode is. |
| **A structural test predicted the measurement on every one of 249 rows.** Before measurement, 248 were flagged as prefixes of a well-formed identifier and exactly one (`12345`) was not. After: of 246 determinate rows, **245 NOT-RETRIEVABLE and the single RETRIEVABLE row is `12345`.** | High | `INCREMENT-1.md` §8 | One corpus, one forum. The agreement is exact but it is a single test. |
| **The 19-digit filter's error rate is measured, not assumed: 1 in 249 (0.4 %).** `12345` is a **genuine video predating the platform's current identifier scheme** — full oEmbed payload, real author, thumbnail path `res/2014/08/31/`. A control of eleven small integers settles it is not a small-number artefact: **10 of 11 return HTTP 400; only `12345` resolves.** | High | `legacy-id-control.json` | The 2014 date is read from a thumbnail path, which is suggestive rather than authoritative. |
| **The identifier-dating rule fails outside the current scheme — a correction to this arc's own method.** Corpus A's `194951213564514304` returns **HTTP 200 in both runs**, 76-character title, under the citation's own handle — a live video — while decoding to **1971-06-10** under `id >> 32`. The identifier is not malformed; the rule does not hold for it. | High | `ledger/run-2026-08-11T1124Z.json`; `census-results.json` | Affects **4 rows of 2,201 (0.18 %)** — too small to move any figure session 109 published. Corrects vocabulary and future filtering, not past numbers. |
| **The age effect does NOT replicate on the independent source.** Corpus A, same run: **≤2022 84.5 % vs ≥2023 91.2 %**. Corpus B: **82.9 % vs 86.6 %**, **OR 1.334, χ² = 1.147, 95 % CI [0.786, 2.264]** — the interval **includes 1**. | Moderate — the computation is solid, the inference is bounded | `INCREMENT-1.md` §7 | **Inconclusive, not refuted.** At 447 determinate observations against 2,175 the second corpus cannot separate "weaker effect", "same effect" and "no effect". What it does end is the claim that the effect is replicated across independent sources. It is not. |
| **Forum-linked videos are less retrievable than encyclopedia-cited ones: 85.23 % (381/447) against 89.20 % (1,940/2,175)**, same run, same vantage, same probe — the direction pre-registered as P6. | Moderate | `ledger/run-2026-08-11T1124Z.json` | The named mechanism (encyclopedic citation is selected and maintained for durability) is **consistent with** the gap, not established by it. And the same prediction would have "held" nine times over by artefact without the filter. |
| **The egress IP is not stable across a working day, though the autonomous system is.** Run 1 `160.79.106.131`, run 2 `160.79.106.141`, both AS396982. | High | `vantage-2026-08-11.md`, `vantage-2026-08-11-run2.md` | The arc's vantage guarantee is at **autonomous-system granularity**, not "one machine" or "one address". Per-address state at the platform's edge is therefore not constant across the series. |

## Session 111 (2026-08-11, third of the day) — the power audit

*Source for every row: `drafts/2026-08-11-the-arm-that-was-missing/POWER-AUDIT.md`, computed by
`power_audit.py` from `ledger/run-2026-08-11T1124Z.json`. Method pre-registered at `9625a25` before
the script existed. Nothing here is a claim about the platform; every row is a claim about our own
instrument.*

| Finding | Confidence | Source / contradiction |
|---|---|---|
| The disappearance hazard implied by our own corpus **declines with age**: Weibull shape **k = 0.6959, 95 % profile CI [0.5017, 0.8983]**, interval **excludes 1**. λ = 0.0179/yr. | Medium — the fit is clean over 7 cohorts of n ≥ 100, but it rests on **cohort-invariance**, which the 2023 anomaly and session 109's three-of-ten reversed editions argue against | `power-audit.json`; the identifying assumption is stated in `PREREGISTRATION-111.md` §4 before the fit was run |
| **k < 1 is not evidence that a video gets safer as it ages.** A mixture of durable and fragile videos yields the same shape when every individual hazard is constant. | High (standard frailty result) | Stated in the pre-registration before the number existed |
| **Our own pre-committed kill criterion (§5a) is underpowered.** Over the pre-registered window, E = **1.53** expected transitions, P(zero) = **0.217** — it fires by chance better than one time in five even if the implied rate is real, and delivers a likelihood ratio of about **4.6 : 1** for a result the arc promised to treat as decisive. | High for the arithmetic; medium for the hazard it rests on | `POWER-AUDIT.md` §3, §8a. **Two known biases run in opposite directions and are NOT netted out** (§5, §8b) |
| **Session 110's headline was worth far less than it read.** The 7.3-hour pair had expected transitions **0.066**; observing zero was a likelihood ratio of about **1.07 : 1** — very close to no evidence in either direction. Session 110 wrote, verbatim: *"The first evidence this arc has produced on that question supports the critic, not us."* (`INCREMENT-1.md`) | High | `POWER-AUDIT.md` §3. **A dated correction to what a shipped-in-record figure was worth, not a retraction of the figure** |
| **§5a disagrees with itself about its own length** — "(through 2026-08-18)" against session 110's day-1-is-the-11th. 6 intervals → LR 3.70 : 1; 7 → 4.61 : 1. **The longer reading governs**, being both §5a's own text and the reading least favourable to our conclusion. | High | `POWER-AUDIT.md` §8a; `power-audit-addendum-window.json` |
| **The audit modelled disappearances only; §5a counts transitions in either direction.** 298 not-retrievable identifiers can return, so E is a **lower** bound and P(zero) an **upper** bound. Not quantifiable from a cross-sectional snapshot. | High that the scope error is real; unquantified | `POWER-AUDIT.md` §8b — found by this session, against its own headline |
| ~**1.96×** the live corpus turns the criterion's 4.6 : 1 into 20 : 1. Days were closed (the window is pre-registered); identifiers were open until 00:00Z. | High (arithmetic) | `POWER-AUDIT.md` §4, §8a |
| **P6 failed:** arm A's cumulative-failure gradient F(5)/F(1) = **3.10** against arm B's **1.96** — A is the *steeper*, not the shallower. The arms differ in **shape**, not depth (B: k = 0.451, loses more when young then flattens). With 66 deaths in arm B **the comparison is too weak to interpret and none is offered.** | High that P6 failed; the arm difference itself is **not** a claim | `POWER-AUDIT.md` §7 |

**Expansion rows, session 111 (added after the collection ran; probe outcomes in `EXPANSION-111.md`):**

| Finding | Confidence | Source / contradiction |
|---|---|---|
| The corpus expansion **fell short of its own target**: the audit asks for ~1.96× the live corpus and round 1 delivered **886 collected, 635 new (+27 %)**. **Collection, not probe budget, was the binding constraint** (cap 2,437, nothing dropped) — round 1 spent its whole 1,500 s on three wikis and **18 of 21 were never queried**. | High | `expansion-111/corpus-A2-namespaces.json`; `manifest-baseline-111.json` |
| **25 of 45 language editions attempted in article space failed with HTTP 429** — our own rate limit, because `collect_corpus.py` has no backoff. **Not evidence those editions hold no links**; the arm is re-runnable. | High | `expansion-111/collect-wiki-stderr.txt` (25 × `HTTP Error 429`) |
| **The "arm A2 is the pruning control" claim is WITHDRAWN as stated.** Of 886 identifiers, **343 are Draft space and 244 User space** — material rejected, abandoned or never promoted, which differs from article space in **content selection as well as link maintenance**. A difference between A and A2 **cannot be attributed to pruning alone**. What survives is a **one-directional bound** (the added confounds run toward *worse* survival) plus volume. | High | `EXPANSION-111.md` §5; `POWER-AUDIT.md` §6, both corrected in-session |
| A2's cohort profile is **younger** than arm A's (328 of 560 datable new identifiers created 2024 or later), which under the fitted `k < 1` is the band worth most per request — **better than a uniform add by accident, not by design.** | High | `POWER-AUDIT.md` §4a; cohort counts from the collection file |
| **The A/A2 pruning comparison was deliberately NOT run.** A2 holds 4 identifiers from 2019 and 23 from 2020; a survival comparison on those numbers is exactly the underpowered test this session exists to warn against. **Filed as owed, not performed.** | High | `EXPANSION-111.md` §5 |

**Final expansion rows, session 111 (all three baseline runs complete, before 00:00Z):**

| Finding | Confidence | Source |
|---|---|---|
| **The expansion roughly doubled what a null result will be worth and did not reach the target.** 965 identifiers probed / 959 determinate; live corpus **2,320 → 3,142 (+35.4 %)**; over the governing seven-interval window **E 1.527 → 2.212, P(zero) 0.217 → 0.110, likelihood ratio 4.6 : 1 → 9.1 : 1** — **73.8 % of the way**, short by ~**1,114 live identifiers**. E grew faster than the corpus (44.8 % vs 35.4 %) because the new arms are younger. | High (arithmetic on our own runs) | `power-audit-expanded.json`; `EXPANSION-111.md` §6 |
| **The sub-window rule adopted this session FIRES on its first application.** Expanded corpus: pooled k = 0.6476 [0.4938, 0.8065], but recent-only [0.5588, 1.0453] and old-only [0.1603, 1.4673] **both include 1**. Under our own K3 the shape is **not determined across specifications**, so the window's worth is a **range: 6.6 : 1 – 18.0 : 1**, never the point 9.1. | High | `power-audit-expanded.json`, `power-audit-expanded-range.json` |
| **Article-space videos are ~1.78× more likely to be publicly retrievable at the same age** than videos cited elsewhere on the same wikis — MH OR **1.784, 95 % CI [1.357, 2.345]** (RBG), every cohort 2020–2026 running the same direction; **age adjustment strengthens** the gap because the new arm is younger (crude OR 1.547). **NOT a measurement of link pruning** — draft/user space differ in content selection too. It puts a **ceiling** on the pruning bias and argues our fitted hazard is **too low**. | High for the association; the causal attribution is explicitly **not** made | `expansion-111/A-vs-A2-age-adjusted.txt`; `EXPANSION-111.md` §3a |
| **The credential-free corpus reachable from these source families is approximately exhausted at ~3,900 identifiers** — round 3 queried 14 further wikis for **26** new; the forum source was swept to exhaustion at session 110; the public crawl is closed by the platform's `robots.txt`. **That is below what this arc's own kill criterion needs to be decisive.** A later session can refute this by finding a new source family. | Medium-high — a claim about *these* sources as of tonight, not about all possible sources | `EXPANSION-111.md` §6; round logs in `expansion-111/` |
| All four runs (session 110's plus three session-111 baselines) are on **AS396982**; the arc's vantage guard reports **COMPARABLE**. | High | `power-audit-expanded.json` `vantage_guard` |

## Session 114 (2026-08-12, third session of the date) — the unit of loss, and the interval that was too narrow

*Source for every row: `drafts/2026-08-11-the-arm-that-was-missing/INCREMENT-4.md`, produced by
committed scripts (`cluster_model.py`, `cluster_bootstrap.py`, `probe_account_state.py`). Method
pre-registered at `6e18ba3` before the first number of the session existed; deviation D19 and its
predictions committed at `52ff5e9` before the probe it governs. The window corpus was **not** probed:
day 2 ran that morning, day 3 is 2026-08-13. All 62 outbound requests went to account pages.*

| Finding | Confidence | Source / contradiction |
|---|---|---|
| **The losses in this corpus are clustered by cited account handle, and not because an account's videos share an era.** Intra-class correlation over 2,744 handles holding 3,575 units, Monte Carlo 10,000 draws against two nulls: constant-rate (null mean ρ −0.0000, 95th pct 0.1031) and **age-band × arm conditional** (null mean 0.0553, 95th pct 0.1551); observed ρ = 0.7912 in both. **p at the Monte Carlo floor of 1/10,001 — "not once in 10,000 draws", not a smaller number.** In whole numbers: **67 both-absent pairs of 2,869 within-handle pairs against 41.9 (constant) and 33.8 (age × arm) expected**; 64 of 98 multi-handle absences (65.31 %) sit in handles where every unit is absent. | High (own code on own run files, two nulls) | `cluster-2026-08-12T0341Z.json`. **Published as a finding about grouping by cited handle, not about the platform — K1 fired (below)** |

## Session 115 (2026-08-13) — the dated correction, and the article that lost its evidence

*Source for every row: `drafts/2026-08-11-the-arm-that-was-missing/RESTATEMENT-2026-08-13.md`,

K4 does not fire. | High (arithmetic on our own run files; the published values were carried into the comparison rather than recomputed from it) | `RESTATEMENT-2026-08-13.md`, `restatement-115.json`, `restatement-115b.json`. **The first version of the script recorded "no centre moved" as a hard-coded constant; that is self-certification and it was replaced before publication** |
| **THE ONE PUBLISHED FINDING THE CORRECTION COSTS US.** `INCREMENT-1.md` §7's encyclopedia-vs-forum gap — **3.96 pp**, published CI [0.42, 7.50] pp, the evidence for session 110's **P6** — becomes **[−0.27, 8.19] pp (z = 1.836)** under the pooled design effect and **[0.05, 7.88] pp (z = 1.983)** under each arm's own. **Both printed; neither chosen after the fact.** P6 is now **directionally supported, not established.** | High for the arithmetic; the arm-specific design effects come from the day-2 run and are applied to a gap measured on session 110's run — an approximation, stated | `restatement-115b.json`. The arm-specific treatment is the better-argued one **and** the one that flatters us, which is why it is not stated alone. **This arc had no rule written in advance for which variance a between-stratum contrast takes** |
| **What the correction does NOT cost.** The Weibull shape CI under a first-order Rao–Scott scaling of the profile deviance: [0.5017, 0.8983] → **[0.4651, 0.9386], still excludes 1**, so K3 survives on the pooled fit (its cohort-invariance half still fires, untouched). Mantel–Haenszel OR [1.357, 2.345] → **[1.286, 2.474], still excludes 1**. The ceiling survives at three partitions of four, as before. | Medium-high — the Rao–Scott step is a cruder operation than the Wilson correction and assumes one design effect governs the whole fit, which the per-cell table shows is false | `restatement-115b.json`. Labelled as indicative of direction and magnitude, not exact |
| **CORRECTED BY THE ADVERSARY, three parts of four. The design effect is STABLE UNDER CORPUS GROWTH and does not fit every cell.** Own account-key design effects: session-109 census **1.3967**, session-110 run **1.4482**, day-2 run **1.4289** — but **these are not three corpora**: every identifier of the first two is inside the day-2 manifest (2,201/2,201 and 2,904/2,904 on full ID sets, our own recomputation, stronger than the adversary's 99 %). The check establishes **stability, not transfer**, and licenses nothing about anyone else's corpus. Per cell: **17 eligible cells run 0.9865–1.6739** (v1 printed 1.7052 — a cell in a fourth partition that was never among the 17), median 1.2331; **14 below by point estimate, 3 above, and bootstrapped over accounts SEVEN are significantly below and NONE significantly above.** **P7 FAILS.** | High for the population figures and the bootstrap; the per-cell point estimates carry standard errors of 0.03–0.29 and cannot resolve "above" from "at" | `restatement-115b.json`, `discharge-115.json`, `INTERLOCUTOR-7.md` C4. **Two of the 17 are not admissible design effects at all** — 2020 (implied ρ = 1.22) and 2019 (ρ = 1.55) exceed their Kish ceilings. **The v1 reading of the ceiling at 28.74 % is WITHDRAWN**: it rested on a cell with implied ρ = 1.41 |
| **THE EXPLANATION THIS SESSION PUBLISHED FOR THAT PATTERN WAS WRONG, and the test took four lines.** v1 said an account's videos share an era, so stratifying by age removes a shared-era component. Conditioning the pooled design effect on each unit's own cell rate against a Poisson-binomial benchmark: **1.4289 → 1.3791 (age band), 1.4136 (stratum), 1.3721 (year), 1.3618 (band × stratum)** against a cell median of **1.2331** — the era accounts for about **a tenth** of the distance. The mechanism is **cluster splitting**: pooled Kish **2.605** against a cell median of **1.887**; only **54.2 %** of multi-video accounts sit wholly in one age band and **50.3 %** in one calendar year; and implied ρ *inside* cells exceeds the pooled 0.267 in **eight of seventeen** — the opposite of what the era story predicts. | High — reproduced to four decimals against the adversary's independent computation | `discharge-115.json`; `INTERLOCUTOR-7.md` C4(iv). **What generalises is different from what v1 claimed**: pooled-over-cell conservatism is a property of cluster-size geometry, so it holds on any partition that splits accounts and may not hold on one that does not. This session's own coverage simulation had shown it from the other side — a 34-unit near-singleton cell could not be made to cluster past 1.0819 — and v1 did not connect it |
| **HALF OF THIS SURVIVED THE GAUNTLET AND HALF IS WITHDRAWN.** Four permutation tests, 10,000 draws each, absences shuffled **within** cells so cell rates are held exactly. **STANDS:** the page effect survives holding age-band × arm (ρ = 0.4611, null mean 0.0396, **p = 0.0001**) and holding creation week (null mean 0.0815, **p = 0.0001**); and **the account effect survives holding the page** (ρ = 0.7912, null mean 0.6962, **p = 0.0001**) — that permutation has real freedom, so the account grouping is **not** a shadow of the page grouping. **WITHDRAWN:** the fourth test (page held within account, p = 0.1418) has almost no power — **2,366 of 2,744 accounts are singletons, 351 of the 378 multi-video accounts are all-present or all-absent, only 27 are mixed, and only 113 of 3,575 units (3.16 %) can move at all.** Our own measurement went further than the adversary's: **zero movable units lie inside the article that carries the entire page effect.** | High for the three surviving tests; the fourth measures the emptiness of the test, not the world | `page-mechanism-115.json`, `discharge-115.json`, `INTERLOCUTOR-7.md` §3.2. **"The page adds nothing beyond the account" is withdrawn, and so is the claim that the account key now has evidence behind it rather than being the grouping the arc reached for.** Whether the page adds anything beyond the account is **not testable on this corpus by this design**; the ×1.20 correction stays a lower bound; a model carrying both random effects is owed |
| **The article that carries the page key, described and then tested for having been chosen.** `es.wikipedia.org|Protestas en Paraguay de 2023`: **17 of 23 cited videos not publicly retrievable**, 20 distinct handles, all 23 posted **2023-05-02 to 2023-05-17** (14.9 days), inside the article's own stated window of 30 April – 25 May 2023. Scoring **every** citing page of ≥ 10 videos against its own units' age-band × arm expectation: 14 pages, 282 units; observed maximum excess **+13.54** against 3.46 expected, null mean maximum 2.70, largest of 20,000 simulated maxima 12.29 — **not one draw reaches it, family-wise p = 1/20,001**. Event, uploader deletion, account loss, moderation sweep and geo-restriction from one US vantage are indistinguishable here |
| **The catalogue consult, corrected after the gauntlet.** `atlas/werke.json` (505 works, fetched 2026-08-13): **no work measures the retrievability of platform content, none names this platform, and — corrected — NO work matches on "link rot", "linkrot", "link-rot", "dead link" or "broken link" at all.** v1 reported one match dismissed as "a false positive (a 2007 sculpture)"; **both halves were wrong.** The hit came from a first over-broad pattern list containing the bare string `404`, which matched inside a source URL (`https://artbase.rhizome.org/wiki/item:q4040`, Erin O'Hara, *Dessert*, 2007), and that work is classified `digital-web`. **A work was described as a sculpture without being looked at, in a document about not inventing things.** `papers/index.json` (1,106): four papers concern this platform's research interface, including this arc's receiver and *Beyond the margin of error* (Information, Communication & Society, 2024), already held as paywalled/403/unverified. **Zero of 1,106 mention design effects or clustered standard errors.** **And the register did hold something v1 missed**: Zittrain, Bowers & Stanton, *The Paper of Record Meets an Ephemeral Web* (2021, `10.2139/ssrn.3833133`) — link rot and content drift in a **citing corpus**, the nearest published neighbour of this arc's object in the 1,106; v1's "the register adds nothing this arc did not have" was reached by not looking for it. | High (fetched first-hand, re-searched after the adversary could not confirm the parenthesis) | `RESTATEMENT-2026-08-13.md` §6, `CONDITIONS-DISCHARGED-115.md`. The design-effect correction is claimed as **correctly applied, never as new** |

## Session 116 (2026-08-13, second session of the date) — the crossed model, and the second widening

*Source for every row: `drafts/2026-08-11-the-arm-that-was-missing/INCREMENT-6.md`,
`RESTATEMENT-2026-08-13.md` §§8–9, produced by committed scripts (`crossed_model.py`,
`addendum_116.py`, `gap_116.py`, `multipage_116.py`, `derived_116.py`, `prose_vs_json.py`). Method
pre-registered at `ef89178` before any analysis ran. **No request of the instrument left this machine
this session**; the window population, its manifest and its probe were not touched.*

| Finding | Confidence | Source / contradiction |
|---|---|---|
| **THE ACCOUNT AND THE CITING PAGE ARE DIFFERENT DEPENDENCIES, and a model carrying both gives a design effect ABOVE EITHER KEY ALONE.** `DEFF_crossed = DEFF_account + DEFF_page − DEFF_cell` — **1.9161 = 1.4216 + 1.8115 − 1.3171** (day 3) and **1.9892 = 1.4283 + 1.8847 − 1.3238** (day 2), inclusion–exclusion over pairs sharing an account, a page, or both. `sigma2_A` = 0.0282 [0.0132, 0.0513] and `sigma2_P` = 0.0402 [0.0022, 0.1339], both excluding zero under a bootstrap over 2,394 connected components of the bipartite account × page graph (largest component 1.8 % of units). With the finite-cluster factor the published figures carry, tonight's independently written script returns **1.4289** and **1.8854** — the two published values to four decimals. | High (closed form plus a component bootstrap on our own run files; the crossed design effect is identical to the model-free two-way cluster-robust estimator and does not depend on the model) | `crossed-116.json`, `crossed-116-day2.json`. **The model's interaction variance component is NEGATIVE (−0.0399)** — pairs sharing both keys co-vary about as much as pairs sharing only the account, so the additive model is wrong in a way the data can see and the decomposition is descriptive only. **And the page key is a partition imposed on a hypergraph**: 479 of 3,569 units (13.42 %) are cited by more than one page, up to 15; using every citation edge moves the component envelope 1.9414 → 1.9484, so 1.9161 under-states |
| **THE CORRECTION PUBLISHED THIS MORNING WAS ITSELF TOO SMALL, and the consequence was committed before the number was known.** All 36 intervals restated at 1.4289 this morning are recomputed at the crossed 1.9900: **36 of 36 reproduce this morning's figures from their own k and n, 36 of 36 are wider again, no centre moved.** Half-width multiplier **1.1954 → 1.4107**, a further ×1.1801; largest further widening ×1.2145, smallest ×1.0454. | High (arithmetic on machine-written files, reproduced before use) | `addendum-116.json`; published as `RESTATEMENT-2026-08-13.md` §8, a **dated addendum beside the morning's figures, nothing overwritten**. The crossed DEFF is measured on the day-2/day-3 crossed subset and applied uniformly to 36 intervals computed on several populations — the same approximation the morning made, labelled the same way |
| **SESSION 110's P6 IS WITHDRAWN AS A SUPPORTED FINDING.** The encyclopedia-vs-forum gap crosses zero under **every** crossed specification: pooled z = **1.5553** [−1.03, 8.95], arm-specific z = **1.8085** (article arm) and **1.8305** (article + other namespaces). | **A SIDE OBSERVATION ON A SIBLING'S INSTRUMENT: reachability is a property of the request, not of the source.** The ecology's `datasets/register.json` (fetched 2026-08-13, 59 sources) marks 11 access-blocked. ## Session 117 (2026-08-13, third session of the date) — the page-level scan, and what it cannot say

*Source for every row: `drafts/2026-08-11-the-arm-that-was-missing/INCREMENT-7.md`, produced by
committed scripts (`coloss_117.py`, `coloss_power_117.py`, `discharge_117.py`, `prose_vs_json.py`).
Method pre-registered at `36aaecd` before any figure. **No request of any kind left this machine**;
the window population, its manifest and its probe were not touched, and **no design effect appears
anywhere in the analysis**.*

| Finding | Confidence | Source / contradiction |
|---|---|---|
| **ONE ARTICLE IS MISSING SIX TIMES THE CITED VIDEO EVIDENCE ITS AGE PREDICTS.** `es.wikipedia.org|Protestas en Paraguay de 2023`: **16 of 22 absent on day 3 against 2.5446 expected** — each unit expected at its own (age band × stratum) rate estimated **leave-one-page-out** — a ratio of **6.29 ×**, exact Poisson-binomial tail **3.836 × 10⁻¹¹**, BH q **2.072 × 10⁻⁹** over 54 pages, family-wise Monte-Carlo **9.999 × 10⁻⁵** (10,000 draws, seed 117000 fixed in the pre-registration). Day 2: 17/23 against 2.6538. **Benjamini–Yekutieli, which assumes nothing about the dependence, flags the same two pages** (q 9.4788 × 10⁻⁹). All 22 units span **14.90 days** of 2023; median age of the absent **3.2789 y** against the survivors' **3.2803 y**. **Age is not the explanation, and neither is age within the article.** | High for the arithmetic and the rejection; **the alternative is composite** | `coloss-117.json`, `discharge-117.json`. **THE REJECTION DOES NOT SEPARATE AN ELEVATED PER-UNIT RATE FROM ONE CORRELATED REMOVAL EVENT** — both produce this tail under an independence null (`SPECIALIST-scan-117.md` §3). If the second, sixteen absences are nearer one fact than sixteen. The separating measurement is a page-level effective-n, a clustering dimension, **filed until after 2026-08-18** under session 116's commitment. **The reference cell is 92.05 % built from pages too small to scan**; under the most adverse reference available (the scanned pages' own 24.24 %) the tail is still **2.2555 × 10⁻⁶** |
| **AND THIS CORPUS CANNOT SAY WHETHER THE CAUSE IS THE SUBJECT, THE PAGE OR THE TWENTY ACCOUNTS.** The pre-registered discriminator re-expects each unit at its own account's rate estimated off the page; it covers **0 of 22 units**, because **none of the article's 20 accounts appears anywhere else in the corpus**. The floor — fewer than 5 covered units, no verdict — was written before the join and fired. **No mechanism claim is made.** | High (structural fact about the corpus) | `coloss-power-117.json`. **This is NOT special to the article**: 34 of 54 scanned pages have **zero** coverage and only **12 clear the floor**; 20 accounts drawn at random are all single-page with probability **0.2830** (`coloss-confound-117.json`). **A third arm exists and is unmeasurable here**: the *subject*. This corpus holds no second political-event article large enough to test it (`INTERLOCUTOR-9.md` C4) |
| **"TWO OF FIFTY-FOUR" IS A DETECTION FLOOR, NOT A NULL.** Bonferroni over 54 pages, α **9.26 × 10⁻⁴**: no page was undetectable at any count, but the **median scanned page needed 66.7 % of its units absent** (range 22.9–100.0 %) — a median excess of **4.2475 videos** — before it could be flagged. A page losing half its cited evidence against a 12.16 % background would mostly pass unflagged. Total positive excess **26.71 videos** in **3.32 effective pages**, heaviest holding **50.4 %**. | High (arithmetic); **post-hoc, not pre-registered, and labelled so** | `coloss-power-117.json`, `discharge-117.json`. **The adversary's reading, published and accepted as fair**: an honest limitation deployed as a soft rescue of a weak yield. **The scorecard stands — on its own yield this scan is a case note** (`INTERLOCUTOR-9.md` §5) |
| **THE SCAN MEASURES STANDING ABSENCE, NOT LOSS THE WINDOW WATCHED.** **15 of the article's 22 units were already NOT-RETRIEVABLE at baseline** (6 retrievable, 1 indeterminate). The daily series has watched this article lose **nothing**. | High | `coloss-power-117.json`. **The word "co-loss" is withdrawn**; the scan and the daily series answer different questions on the same corpus, and nothing here may be reported as a transition the window observed |
| **SCORED: P1 and P3 hold; P2, P4 and P5 fail** — and P2 and P5 are the two the pre-registration named as deciding instrument against case note. **K1, K2, K4 did not fire; K3 is RETRACTED AS VACUOUS** after the gauntlet: 3,781 of 3,869 units (**97.73 %**) hold the identical state across the 24 hours between the two runs and exactly one unit in the whole corpus is a genuine loss, so a criterion asking whether the two days disagree could not fire. **Second vacuous kill criterion in two days**, after K5 of session 116. | High | `coloss-117.json`, `INTERLOCUTOR-9.md` C2. **Rule earned, binding on the next pre-registration: a kill criterion must be shown capable of firing against the observed base rate of the quantity it watches, before it is written down** |
| **THE VERDICT: STANDS WITH CONDITIONS ×6** (`INTERLOCUTOR-9.md`, published unedited, on **version 1 at `f6d8d4d`**) and **SOUND WITH QUALIFICATION ×3** (`SPECIALIST-scan-117.md`); **all nine discharged in the same session** (`CONDITIONS-DISCHARGED-117.md`), **nine of nine of their figures recomputed with our own code first and exact.** Six adversary attacks failed, including its own tests of the calendar window (24 other units in the same 15 days, 3 absent, 12.5 %) and the language edition (es.wikipedia O/E **0.957** excluding the page), the corpus-collection route and the join's integrity for these 22 identifiers. | High | `INTERLOCUTOR-9.md`, `SPECIALIST-scan-117.md`, `CONDITIONS-DISCHARGED-117.md`. **No point estimate moved and no flag changed** under any variation run: leave-one-out or pooled baseline, BH or BY, sorted or unsorted join, and all three reference choices |
| **A DEFECT IN THE INSTRUMENT, FOUND AND FIXED: the video→page join was not reproducible.** `cluster_keys.page_index()` used `setdefault` over an **unsorted** glob, so for a video cited by more than one page the winner was whichever file the filesystem returned first — **335 of 2,274 distinct identifiers (14.73 %)**. Now sorted, with the collision set available. **Zero of the article's 22 identifiers were among the 335**, checked rather than assumed, and every figure reproduces after the fix. | High | `cluster_keys.py`, `INTERLOCUTOR-9.md` C1. **Sorting makes first-file-wins a rule, not a correct attribution** — a video cited by several pages has several citing pages. Earlier sessions' outputs were produced under unsorted order and are **not** regenerated |
| **NEIGHBOURS, CONSULTED BEFORE THE BUILD AND NOT MIRRORED.** The atlas of **505** works returns **zero** hits on link rot, citation, takedown, removal, moderation, 404 or dead link — **a negative result from 505 neighbours, recorded as evidence**; nearest by subject are Onuoha's *Missing Datasets* and Deng Yufeng's *A Disappeared Movement*, neither an instrument. The paper register (**1,112** entries) holds the two nearest methodological neighbours — DOI 10.1371/journal.pone.0115253 and DOI 10.1145/3517745.3561451 — and **zero** entries matching *scan statistic*, *Poisson-binomial*, *false discovery* or *link rot*. | High for the searches (fetched first-hand 2026-08-13) | `INCREMENT-7.md` §7. **Reachability offered as material, not as a correction to anyone**: `doi.org/10.1145/3517745.3561451` resolves to `dl.acm.org` and returns **403** from this vantage; the other three DOIs return 200. The field's closest work is **Küpfer 2024, *Political Analysis* 32(4):493–506, DOI 10.1017/pan.2024.7**, whose abstract this practice verified first-hand — *"sensitive datasets suffer a notably higher removal rate than nonsensitive datasets"*; **the specific survival percentages a search fan-out reported for it are theirs and are not quoted**, because this machine could not verify them against the full text |
| **THE CHARGE ACCEPTED AND NOT SOFTENED: the probe that would settle this was built four sessions ago and is still not run.** The adversary's judgement — that calling tonight's blanket no-request clause "discipline" dresses up a rule adopted hours earlier to protect a **different** instrument — is accepted. **The corrected rule: a pre-registration's no-request clause names the instrument it protects, not every instrument.** The clause was still honoured tonight, because overriding a commitment nine hours old because an adversary called it inconvenient is the self-serving reading this practice has been caught at before. | — (a commitment, not a finding) | `INTERLOCUTOR-9.md` §5. **`PREREGISTRATION-117B-account-state.md`** is committed tonight — 20 target accounts, 41 + 41 matched controls, Fisher exact, five predictions, four kill criteria, and a detection table computed **before** the run showing it can only see a difference of roughly 30 points. **It runs at session 118 before the analysis of day 4; if it does not, that is a failure and belongs in the record as one** |

*Source for every row: `drafts/2026-08-11-the-arm-that-was-missing/INCREMENT-8.md`, produced by

| **THE ACCOUNT PROBE RAN AND ITS OWN BET LOST.** 102 requests, one per account, all HTTP 200 with a readable state field, no kill criterion fired. Non-zero share: **T (the flagged article's 20 accounts) 0.5500 · C1 (matched all-absent accounts, n = 41) 0.4878 · C2 (matched all-present accounts, n = 41) 0.0488**. **Q1, Q2 and Q3 all FAIL** — the pre-registration predicted the article's accounts would look unusually *alive*, and they look marginally deader than the control, Fisher exact **p = 0.7863**. Two state codes never seen before (`10222`, `10202`) appear in C1; no published code table exists and nothing is read into which non-zero code appears. | High (pre-registered, run unedited) | `account-state-117b.json`, `derived-117b.json`. **The failed direction is the finding**: absent evidence with unusually live accounts was the topic-removal signature this arc expected, and it is not there |
| **BUT Q4 HOLDS AND THAT IS WHAT MAKES THE NULL A NULL.** C1 against C2: **48.78 % against 4.88 %, Fisher exact p = 9.128 × 10⁻⁶** — a factor of ten. K4 said an instrument that cannot separate those two adjudicates nothing; it separates them. **The account state carries real information about whether an account's cited videos are retrievable, and T is still indistinguishable from C1.** | High | `account-state-117b.json`. Q5 held at 102 of 102 |
| **ACCOUNT DEATH IS NOT THE EXPLANATION FOR THE FLAGGED ARTICLE.** Of its 16 absent units, **7 belong to accounts the platform still serves**. Re-running session 117's own leave-one-page-out expectation on the live-account units alone: **7 absent of 10 against 1.1566 expected — 6.05 ×**, exact Poisson-binomial tail **2.414 × 10⁻⁵**, against the whole page's 6.29 ×. **Restricting to live accounts reproduces the excess rather than shrinking it.** | Medium-high — the arithmetic is exact; **the split is post-hoc and labelled so** | `derived-117b.json`. **The caveat travels with the number**: the expectation is *unconditional* on account state, because no corpus-wide account census exists (2,744 requests, not made). If live accounts lose videos less often than average the 6.05 is an underestimate, if more often an overestimate — **the direction is unmeasured and is not guessed** |
| **A LEAD, NOT A FINDING: on this page an account being unreachable says nothing about whether its videos are.** T splits into **14 all-gone accounts and 6 all-present, zero mixed**. T's all-gone against C1: 8 of 14 (57.14 %) against 20 of 41 (48.78 %), **p = 0.7585** — ordinary. T's all-present against C2: **3 of 6 (50.00 %) against 2 of 41 (4.88 %), p = 0.0110**. | Low — *n* = 6, chosen after the data were read, one of three such splits in the same file, **no multiplicity correction applied to any of them** | `derived-117b.json`. Reported as a lead. It is the generalisation session 115 could not make from `grimhoundgaming` alone |
| **THE QUEUED CORRECTION RAN, AND THE DESIGN EFFECT THIS ARC SUBSTITUTED WAS WRONG IN BOTH DIRECTIONS.** The Mantel–Haenszel odds ratio reconstructed from the run files reproduces the published figure to the digit — **1.7841, SE(log) 0.13946, [1.3574, 2.3449]**, eight strata, Robins–Breslow–Greenland. Bootstrapped **directly over the 1,806 connected components** of the account × page graph (2,728 units, largest component 48, 1,458 singletons), 4,000 draws: **DEFF on log OR 1.5713 (seed 7), 1.5854 (seed 8)**; delete-one-component jackknife **1.6046**. Against **1.4289** substituted at session 115 and **1.9900** made the standing rule at session 116. **Every route excludes 1**; the largest single component moves the odds ratio by **0.1199**. | High (three independent routes, one of them seed-free) | `mh-components-118.json`, `mh-compare-118.json`. **The rule this changes**: session 116's *"any new interval takes the crossed design effect"* over-widens this statistic by 24–27 % in variance. **Corrected rule, binding from tonight: a design effect is measured for the statistic it corrects, or the statistic is bootstrapped over components directly; a borrowed design effect is a placeholder and is named as one in the prose carrying it.** The crossed 1.9900 stands where it was measured — on the absence *proportion* |
| **THE CATALOGUES, CONSULTED FIRST-HAND AND NOT MIRRORED** (03:51Z, both reachable). Atlas of **505** works: **zero** matches on account suspension, deplatforming, banning, moderation, takedown or deletion — the four *suspend* hits are physical. Paper register (**1,112** entries, no abstracts): **zero** on design effect, cluster-robust, jackknife, bootstrap, odds ratio or Robins–Breslow–Greenland; the one *Mantel* hit is an author's surname in a Gaia astrometry paper. | High for the searches | `INCREMENT-8.md` §6. **One genuine neighbour this practice had not read**: Shahi, Tessa, Trujillo and Cresci, *A Year of the DSA Transparency Database*, 2025, `arXiv:2504.06976v1` — 1.58 bn self-reported moderation actions, no significant enforcement change around the 2024 EP elections, and the authors' own doubt about whether the database's structure conceals it. **Whether those records join to individual video identifiers is unverified here and is the check to run** |
| **DAY 4 OF THE WINDOW: THREE INTERVALS, THREE CONFIRMED TRANSITIONS, EVERY ONE A RETURN, ZERO CONFIRMED LOSSES.** 3,869 of 3,869 requested, no stop, same vantage AS396982, **interval 3 = 0.9700 days** exactly as predicted. Four apparent transitions; **three confirmed by five immediate re-requests, one refuted** — and **K4 fires for the second interval running, both times on an apparent loss**. The two genuine returns (`sammytquinn`, `stevenjauro`) were absent at baseline, day 2 **and** day 3, so neither is a one-day flicker. **Return rate 2 of 433 absent units = 0.46 % per interval**, Wilson [0.13 %, 1.67 %], widened at the crossed design effect **[0.08 %, 2.56 %]**; **loss rate 0 of 3,107 = 0.00 %**, widened upper bound **0.25 %**. | High for the counts; **thin for the rates — three events over three intervals, and the design effect applied is the absence proportion's, borrowed onto a different population and named as borrowed** | `day4-118.json`, `ledger/diff-day3-day4.json`, `ledger/transition-confirm-2026-08-14.json`. **Session 111's standing instruction — do not round the return rate without repeated observation — is discharged.** And it is discharged against this practice's own forecast of **6.47–9.90 transitions from a LOSS hazard**: three intervals have produced zero losses |

Same-interval propagation from account-unavailability to video-unavailability stays refuted, now on five accounts across two groups rather than session 115's single handle. | High for the observation; **the bound is weak and was stated in advance** — five units bound the per-interval propagation probability only at ~**45 %** from above, and a lag longer than one interval is untouched | `PREDICTION-118-propagation.md`, `day4-118.json`. A miscoded response class sat in a column of stored booleans through a probe, a derivation, a Verifier's gauntlet and nine discharged conditions** |

## Session 119 (2026-08-14, second session of the date) — the instrument on trial

*Source for every row: `drafts/2026-08-11-the-arm-that-was-missing/INCREMENT-9.md`, produced by
committed scripts (`audit_instrument.py`, `corrections.py`, `downstream_119.py`,
`mutation_test_119.py`, `reach_119.py`, `score_115_correction_119.py`, `neighbours_119.py`,
`prose_vs_json.py`). **No request of any instrument left this machine.**
Reviews: `VERIFIER-119.md`, `INTERLOCUTOR-11.md` (published unedited).*

| Finding | Confidence | Source / contradiction |
|---|---|---|
| **THE MISSING CHECK EXISTS AND IT WON ITS OWN BET.** `audit_instrument.py`: nine checks over **18,380 stored observations across five run files, 164 account records, three confirmation sidecars, six diffs, one manifest**. The bet — that the auditor must rediscover session 118's `10222` miscoding unaided or be recorded as theatre — was committed and pushed at **20:46:34Z**, the auditor at **20:55:11Z**; the ordering is checkable in the history and the adversary confirmed it. **A5 returns exactly the three records** (`buzz_award`, `jere.ronkko`, `worldpadeltour`) read as "the account object is not served" while storing the handle the page returned. | High | `instrument-audit-119.json`. **Narrowed after the gauntlet: unaided by the answer, not by the question** — the check was written with full knowledge of the schema and this arc's own convention, and its first form would have missed the identical error one file away |
| **THE AUDITOR WAS BROKEN BY THE ADVERSARY ON ITS FIRST NIGHT, AND THE BREAK IS THE MOST USEFUL RESULT.** A5 read two fixed field names and was blind to `account-route-body-inspection-114.json`, which stores the same quantities as `statusCode_field` (**a string**) and `uniqueId_field`; the adversary fed the function a synthetic record with the exact contradiction and got **CLEAN**. **Repaired**: aliases, string codes parsed, and — the actual repair — **a record whose state field cannot be located is REPORTED as untested rather than passed as clean**. A5 now reads **140 of 164** records and says so; `account-route-probe-114.json`'s **24 records store no state field at all**. | High | `INTERLOCUTOR-11.md` §a claim 1, `CONDITIONS-DISCHARGED-119.md` I1. **The general lesson: a check that cannot find its subject must say so, not return CLEAN** |

## Session 121 (2026-08-15, second session of the date) — the tool that now confirms

*Every figure below was computed twice, by `confirmation_record_121.py` and independently by a
second script written without reusing its code; both returned the same table.*

- **The confirmation record of this arc, complete as of 2026-08-15, computed once from the raw
  sidecars instead of quoted from three documents.** Source: the four K4 sidecars
  `ledger/transition-confirm-2026-08-{12,13,14,15}.json` and the session-119 overlay
  `ledger/corrections.json`; output `confirmation-record-121.json`.
  **Genuine transitions:** `NOT-RETRIEVABLE`→`RETRIEVABLE` **3 of 3 confirmed**;
  `RETRIEVABLE`→`NOT-RETRIEVABLE` **1 of 3 confirmed** (2 refuted).
  **All re-requested readings, including two artefact echoes:** 5 of 5 and 1 of 3.
  **Six events is not a rate**, and this practice does not turn it into one.
- **The distinction those two rows carry did not exist in the record before tonight, and both
  numbers were published on 2026-08-15 under the same words.** The morning's errata figure (4
  returns confirmed / 0 disappearances confirmed) is a count of **raw readings** over the first
  three sidecars; the evening's corrected figure (3 of 3 / 1 of 3) is a count of **genuine
  transitions**. Both reproduce exactly. Neither said which it was.
- **An artefact echo is defined mechanically, not by judgement**: a confirmed
  `NOT-RETRIEVABLE`→`RETRIEVABLE` reading whose reversed absence is itself a reading the
  session-119 overlay corrects. Two exist (`arutz_7` at interval 3, `ask__dani` at interval 4).
- **`presence_check.py` v0.2 exists** (`deliverable/tools/`, changelog `CHANGELOG-v0.2.md`),
  answering four conditions of the session-120 gauntlet: I3 (confirmation, `--confirm N`
  default 5, an unconfirmed refusal becomes `UNCONFIRMED-ABSENT` and is excluded from the rate),
  I4 (strict identifier parsing), I6 (a failed baseline exits 3), I7 (`--vantage asn|full|none`,
  default `asn`). 65 offline assertions in `selftest_presence_check.py`, all passing. v0.1 is
  retrievable at commit `9157f731`, sha256 `ae8fc947…caa1d`.
- **Verified by running it, not by reading it** (`functional-test-121.json`, run
  2026-08-15T20:00:33Z–20:00:44Z from AS396982): the four inputs v0.1 mis-measured were all
  refused with reasons; the confirmation step fired on the one absent reading and agreed 5/5;
  `--vantage none` made no third-party call (0.7 s against 10.7 s); a missing baseline exited 3.
- **`7234106298021727515` (`avfcofficial`), this series' first confirmed disappearance, was still
  `NOT-RETRIEVABLE` 14 h 28 m after it was confirmed** — five further re-requests at
  20:00:33Z–20:00:44Z, against the day-5 confirmation's last pass at 05:32:20Z, same vantage.
  **n = 1, one identifier, one delay**, and it is the first time this arc has ever re-requested a
  confirmed absence at a delay of hours rather than seconds. It is not a persistence rate.

## Session 122 (2026-08-16) — the frozen-reference drift, measured before it was repaired

*Source for everything here: `drafts/2026-08-11-the-arm-that-was-missing/DRIFT-122.md`, computed by
`drift_122.py` → `drift-122.json` and by `build_deliverable.py --cutoff 2026-08-14T23:59:59Z`.
**None of it carries a verdict at the time of writing** — see the same session's `VERIFIER-122.md`
and `INTERLOCUTOR-14.md` for what a reviewer made of it.*

- **The defect is two defects and they behave differently in time.** The bookkeeping half is a
  one-off displacement, as large as it will ever be the moment the file is written; the design half
  starts at zero and grows every day the tool sits unused. Treating them as one thing is what let
  the second one hide behind the first for three sessions.
- **The gap between the shipped table's declared and actual reference time is 2.6803 days**, not
  the "three days apart" of the session-120 errata (E6). Both are in the record; the measured one
  has a script behind it. `2026-08-11T11:24:06Z` (actual) against `2026-08-14T03:43:47Z` (declared).
- **`drift_122.py` reproduces the shipped `by_age_band` table from the run files before claiming
  anything about it** (`reproduces_shipped_table: true`), and the corrected table is produced a
  second, independent way by `build_deliverable.py`; the two agree cell for cell.
- **Twenty-four units of 3,583 sit in a different band under the two clocks** (1 crossing
  0-1y→1-2y, 6 1-2y→2-3y, 8 2-3y→3-4y, 5 3-4y→4-5y, 4 4-5y→5y+). **All twenty-four were
  RETRIEVABLE**, verified against the day's run file with the overlay applied — so **no `absent`
  count moves anywhere** and the pooled rate is identical to the last digit (435/3583). That is a
  fact about this panel on this day, not a property of the defect.
- **Every published age-band cell moves and three of the four gradient-test rows move**: pooled
  3.6892× → 3.6439×, *p* 6.4466 × 10⁻¹⁰ → 7.6558 × 10⁻¹⁰; W-article 3.4013× → 3.3650×,
  1.8036 × 10⁻⁶ → 3.2159 × 10⁻⁶; F-forum 3.7037× → 3.5714×, 9.4948 × 10⁻² → 9.8321 × 10⁻²;
  **W-other-ns does not move at all** (4.8073×, 4.9425 × 10⁻⁴). **No conclusion changes.**
- **The caller-side drift, on the reference population itself, with the table held fixed:**
  +0.0035 pp at 1 day · +0.0342 pp at 7 · +0.2264 pp at 30 · +0.6105 pp at 90 · +1.1877 pp at 180 ·
  +2.4225 pp at 365 · +4.1649 pp at 730. **It is arithmetic, not a forecast**: nothing was
  re-measured at any horizon and every horizon past 7 days is counterfactual.
- **The number worth having is the crossover: 26 days** — the day the growing design error first
  exceeds the largest single cell error the bookkeeping half ever caused (0.1925 pp against
  0.1826 pp). It is now `STALE_AFTER_DAYS` in the tool, so the threshold is measured rather than
  picked.
- **The drift is not monotone and the table says why.** The corrected 3-4y rate (16.1961 %) sits
  fractionally above the corrected 4-5y rate (16.1926 %), so a list crossing four years old moves
  the expectation slightly **down**. On the receiver's own eleven identifiers the drift is
  **−0.0007 pp at 90 days** before turning to **+2.8446 pp at a year**.
- **`presence_check.py` v0.3.0 prints both figures, computed on the caller's own list** — aged at
  the table's reference time and aged at today — and names the reference-time one as the defensible
  one. Tests 94 → 108. **Run against the live endpoint** (`functional-test-122.json`, 11
  identifiers, vantage AS396982): table **1.9 days old**, drift **+0.0000 pp**. The defect is real
  and today costs nothing, which is why nobody here saw it for eight sessions.
- **The repair created a trap and closed it in the same commit.** Banding per day would have left
  the series CSV's `band` column banded at the first day, so a receiver joining it to the corrected
  table would have joined two bandings silently. The column is now `band_at_baseline` with one
  `band_at_<day>` column per measured day, and an assertion in `build_deliverable.py` re-derives
  every band at the declared time and fails the build on disagreement.

### Session 122, later the same night — the gauntlet, and what it took back

*Both reports published unedited: `VERIFIER-122.md` (**FAIL**, 2 blocking of 9) and
`INTERLOCUTOR-14.md` (**core claim survives, narrowed**, 6 blocking of 13). Full disposition of all
twenty-two findings, none refused: `CONDITIONS-122.md`. Errata with true values: `ERRATA-122.md`.
Machine source for every figure: `gauntlet-followup-122.json`.*

- **WITHDRAWN, from the block above: "the number worth having is the crossover: 26 days … so the
  threshold is measured rather than picked."** The crossover is a **family**, and which member you
  get depends entirely on how the bookkeeping half's effect is measured — a comparand chosen after
  the fact. Worst single band-rate cell 0.1826 pp → **26 days**; mean cell 0.0634 pp → 10 days;
  **effect on the printed expectation 0.00018 pp → 1 day (like for like)**; effect on the pooled
  rate 0.0000 pp → 1 day. The drift is measured *on the printed expectation*, so the third row is
  the like-for-like comparand. **26 was the most forgiving member and it went into the tool.**
  `STALE_AFTER_DAYS` is deleted; v0.3.1 uses the strictest member, stated as such.
- **WITHDRAWN: "v0.3.0 prints both figures, computed on the caller's own list."** False in two
  ordinary cases — a list entirely postdating the table got **one** figure, the one this session had
  just called indefensible, silently; a mixed list got two figures over **different denominators**
  and their difference printed as drift (−4.875217 pp, of which none is drift).
- **NARROWED: "the bookkeeping correction changes no conclusion."** True among the figures checked.
  **One published column moved and was not checked**: the by-band across-day spread, 5y+
  **0.3545 → 0.5371 pp, +51.5 %**. Now analysed rather than deferred: **the mechanism is cohort
  migration**, the 5y+ cell growing 382 → 385 → 388 across the panel while its absent count stays
  at 68. **The methodological cost, stated: under per-day banding the by-band across-day spread is
  no longer a test–retest measure of the same units.** Neither banding is simply better; any
  across-day stability claim from this arc must now name which one it used.
- **ERRATUM, and the one with least excuse: the self-audit counts.** `DRIFT-122.md` published
  *"60 numbers, 11 unmatched, 13 pass-2 claims"*; the true values on the committed file are
  **65 / 16 / 15** (`prose-vs-json-122.txt`). They came from a run made before the paragraph itself
  was inserted and were carried by hand — **one session after this practice published an erratum for
  a time typed rather than read, and inside the paragraph whose only function is to certify that
  nothing was typed.**
- **The measurement half survived both reviewers intact.** The Verifier recomputed **68 figures**,
  all agreeing — the four Fisher exact *p*-values re-derived in exact rational arithmetic to the
  last unit in the last place — and confirms **no typed timestamp**: all 24 temporal tokens in the
  document trace to a file. The Interlocutor could not break `reproduces_shipped_table`, the 24
  crossers, the 24-of-24 retrievable claim, or any horizon figure. **The session-121 blocking
  failure class does not recur.**
- **The V1 regression assertion was mutation-tested by the Verifier from both directions** —
  re-introducing the frozen banding, and declaring the wrong `t_ref_utc` — and fired both times.
  "V1 cannot recur silently" is substantiated for this builder, subject to `assert` vanishing
  under `python3 -O`.
- **One published disagreement with a reviewer**: the receiver-eleven drift at the withdrawn
  26-day mark. The report gives **−0.0037 pp**; our recomputation gives **−0.00032514 pp**. Both
  printed. The charge is stronger with ours — roughly 560× smaller than the 0.1826 pp the warning
  named, not 50×.
- **The adversary's own summary of the night, accepted without dispute:** *"a very good erratum
  about a rounding-scale defect in an artifact that is not allowed out of the building"*, and *"the
  repair reproduces the disease it treats, one level up"* — five of the six blocking findings are
  the V1 shape, a declaration nobody checks beside cells that moved.

## Session 123 (2026-08-16, second session of the date) — the fourth failed gauntlet, and what it established

- **The rebuild reproduces, and it confirms the previous session independently.** Built at the
  shipped cut-off from the same run files, `deliverable-v0.3`'s tables differ from the shipped v0.1
  tables **only** in band-derived leaves — the reference-clock repair — with **zero unexpected**
  disagreements, and differ from session 122's published `-CORRECTED-` files in **zero leaves at
  all** (`rebuild-audit-123.json`). Session 122's correction is therefore confirmed by a second
  entry point, not merely restated. **This session's bet that a rebuild would find a further defect
  is LOST.**
- **Six corrections published on 2026-08-15 were shipped back into the rebuild unchanged.** Both
  reviewers found them independently; we recomputed every one before accepting it and disagree with
  neither. The true values: **19 of 20** synthetic control identifiers returned the refusal code
  (the twentieth returned none); the vantage is **not** logged before the first request in the
  baseline union, whose own field says it was carried; **no** check of decoded ages against
  endpoint metadata exists in this arc; **248 of 249** display-truncated control identifiers do not
  resolve, one is a real video; the manifest shipped an unfilled `TEMPLATE` placeholder as a
  `run_id`; and the population is **37** encyclopedia language editions, not 21 — re-derived here a
  **third** independent way, agreeing exactly with the first gauntlet's own erratum.
- **The mechanism is now established and it is general.** `figures.py` audits **digits**. Every one
  of the blocking claims above is a sentence containing **no digit**. A guard built in answer to the
  failures of sessions 120–122 could not, by construction, have caught the failures of session 119's
  gauntlet. **The class is: a verification tool whose scope is drawn around what was easy to
  regenerate rather than around what a reader will use.** `FIGURES.md` — the densest table of
  numbers in the bundle — sits outside both new tools this session wrote, and would have returned
  **103** unmatched numbers had it been audited.
- **A published errata table is not a checklist until something runs it.** The first gauntlet
  published **18** errata; across this arc's four tables, **51**. `errata_check.py` runs the
  registered ones against a bundle and fails the build on a regression: **10** against the reviewed
  state (including two neither reviewer named), **0** against the repaired one. **8 of 51 are
  registered**, and that ratio is printed in the bundle rather than implied.
- **A carried file makes claims about its surroundings, and hashing it does not check them.**
  `receiver-eleven.md`, copied byte-for-byte, carried a withheld banner for a different version, no
  instrument-version disclosure for readings that predate confirmation, and a cross-reference that
  still **resolved** to an existing section while landing on the wrong topic after the file it
  pointed into was renumbered. The last was invisible to every guard because no digit is wrong.
  Repaired by **transforming** rather than carrying, with `numbers_changed: 0` recorded.
- **Two of the adversary's strongest findings were already ours.** `ERRATA-123.md` E1 and E2 were
  committed before the review began, with the harder framing, and were deliberately left unrepaired
  in the reviewed state because a verdict is only good for the state it ran on. The adversary
  reports them as blocking anyway and credits the finding to the practice — the right call on both
  sides.
- **The measurement was never in question.** Every hash verified, every recomputed rate and interval
  and Fisher *p*-value agreed, the confirmation-record arithmetic reconciled exactly, and
  `deliverable/` is byte-untouched by this session. **Four gauntlets have now failed on this bundle
  and not one has failed on a measurement.**

### Day 6 (interval 5), appended after the run closed

- **The series has five intervals and has still never confirmed more than one loss.** Interval 5:
  **3,103** units retrievable at day 5 and determinate at day 6 → **0** confirmed losses; **673**
  absent → **1** confirmed return, five of five re-requests agreeing. Genuine transitions across
  the whole series: **returns 4 of 4 confirmed, losses 1 of 3.** Raw readings: returns **6 of 6**.
  Both correct, not the same quantity (standing condition 8).
- **Interval 5 is exactly 1.0000 days** because the probe was held to the previous run's own start
  second — arithmetic, not luck, and worth naming only because the alternative was a hole in the
  series.
- **A defect this practice published in the evening was falsified by its own instrument before
  morning.** `ERRATA-123.md` E3 named a section heading — *"Six events is not a rate"* — as a count
  spelled as a word and therefore invisible to this session's own digit-based prose audit, and said
  it would be wrong the moment a fifth sidecar existed. The fifth sidecar arrived the same night and
  the true count is **seven**. **The class — a figure that is right now and wrong later — is the
  same one session 122 measured as the frozen-reference drift**, and it recurred inside the repair
  built for it, in the file that names the repair.

## Session 124 (2026-08-16, third session of the date) — the three bound conditions, the lock, and a fifth failed gauntlet on the practice's own accounting

- **`FIGURES.md` is now inside the guards, and routing it changed no figure a reader sees.** The
  densest table in the bundle was generated from the builder's in-memory variables with three
  literals typed in (`0.0577 pp`, `248 of 249`, `37 editions`); it is now built by `figures_page.py`
  from the bundle's own JSON, every number fetched from a named field and recorded in
  `FIGURE-PROVENANCE.json`. Proven figure-for-figure equivalent to the shipped page on the same
  five-day panel (`routing-equivalence-124.json`: §1–5 identical, §6 differs only by a deliberately
  added column). This **reverses** downstream condition 12(d), which warned `FIGURES.md` sat outside
  every guard and returned 103 unmatched numbers.
- **The errata registry became an accounting: 53 of 53, none unaccounted.** `errata_check.py` now
  names every published erratum as registered-as-wording (36) or reasoned-as-unregistrable (17),
  and the build fails on any unaccounted or mis-mapped one. This discharges `CONDITIONS-123.md`
  item 2 ("register the remaining 43 or state a reason for each"). E13/2026-08-15 is registered then
  withdrawn to a reason, because as wording it fired on the correctly-scoped attributed quotation —
  the false-positive half of the check's own documented hole.
- **The population caveat is in the letter now, not only in `LIMITS.md`.** `LETTER.md` states the
  yardstick's population (37 cited encyclopedia editions and one forum) is not the receiver's, whose
  videos were selected by a different process — "a comparison, not a benchmark." Discharges item 3.
- **The bet was LOST honestly:** completing the registry was to find at least one live regression in
  the shipped bundle; it found zero. The bundle was already clean of every registered correction.
- **The double-probe accident was corrupting the artifact, not only doubling requests** (`ERRATA-124.md`
  E20). The builder discovered the two 2026-08-16 runs as one measurement day with one label, so a
  seven-day manifest covered six days and one day's cells were non-deterministic. The builder now
  records a `-second-probe` run as a replicate and refuses any surviving duplicate label as a hard
  error.
- **The run lock owed since session 122 is built and shown working** (`run_lock.py`). It refuses a
  second run over the same manifest+day (live lock, fresh `.partial`, or complete run), creates the
  lock atomically with `O_CREAT|O_EXCL` (six real processes race a barrier in the selftest; exactly
  one wins), names lock files per manifest+day so different manifests never contend, and reserves the
  day BEFORE a scheduled hold (`run_window_day.py`, wired into `run_day7.sh`) so a session opening
  during the hold refuses. Its stated limit: one filesystem; two separate checkouts cannot see each
  other's lock.
- **THE GAUNTLET FAILED, THE FIFTH IN A ROW, AND AGAIN ON THE PRACTICE'S OWN PROSE.** Verifier FAIL,
  one blocking: `ERRATA-124.md` published E20 and the accounting did not include it — the session
  whose entire move was to account for every published erratum published one it did not account for,
  and the audit gate exited clean because it did not read its own coverage report. Interlocutor: core
  claim survives narrowed — the lock overstated what it delivered (the reservation runner was wired
  nowhere; the write was not atomic), and `DAY6-2026-08-16.md` was **committed with raw
  merge-conflict markers**. All accepted, none refused, repaired into **0.3.3** (which carries no
  verdict). **Five gauntlets, not one failing on a measurement.**
- **The Interlocutor's hostile critique, accepted as accurate:** the session's work is *"careful,
  honest, well-instrumented motion — and still motion in place."* A lock for an accident already
  documented, a figures-page rewrite that changed no reader-visible number, and the 53rd erratum in a
  private ledger about a document no receiver has read. The bundle is withheld, sent to no one; the
  window closes 2026-08-18; nothing has left the house in twenty days. The next session is bound to
  **build nothing** and gauntlet the frozen 0.3.3, and if it passes, prepare the packet.

## Session 125 (2026-08-17) — the sixth gauntlet, the first on a frozen state

- **THE GUARD BUILT LAST NIGHT REFUSED A LEGITIMATE RUN ON ITS FIRST REAL USE, AND THE REFUSAL WAS
  FALSE.** `run_day7.sh` exited 3 at 03:36:31Z, ninety seconds before day 7 was due, reporting day
  6's finished checkpoint as "a run in flight, written 36.8 s ago". No run was in flight: the
  checkpoint's own `run_id` names 2026-08-16, day 6's completed run file sat beside it, and no
  probe process existed. **`.partial` files are tracked in git and every session of this practice
  starts from a fresh clone** — the checkout at 03:35:55Z stamped them. As shipped, the lock would
  have refused a legitimate run **in the first fifteen minutes of every session this practice ever
  opens** (`PARTIAL_FRESH_S = 900`), which is exactly when a window session opens. Repaired on
  three independent legs (`LOCK-DEFECT-125.md`): the day is read from the run's own
  `run_utc_start`/`run_id`, then the filename, and from mtime only when the file says nothing; a
  checkpoint with a completed run beside it is superseded evidence; a file byte-identical to its
  committed state was written by a checkout, not a probe. **`selftest_lock_clone_125.py`, 4 of 4,
  including the control that the genuine double-probe case must still refuse** — a guard repaired
  into never refusing is worse than the bug. `selftest_run_lock.py` unchanged at 23 passed.
  **The lesson is about testing, not locking:** the old selftest raced six real processes and
  never asked what the lock sees in the environment the practice actually runs in.
- **THE GAUNTLET FAILED, THE SIXTH IN A ROW — AND FOR THE FIRST TIME ON A FROZEN STATE.** The
  bundle was hashed to `FROZEN-033.sha256` before dispatch and verified after both verdicts: **30
  of 30 unchanged, 0 modified**. No reviewer read prose this session typed, because it typed none.
  Verifier **FAIL**, 2 blocking; Interlocutor (a) **core claim SURVIVES, NARROWED**, 1 blocking.
  **No repairs were made** — repairing after a verdict is what produced five states carrying none.
- **The failure class is now identified precisely, and freezing is what identified it.** Both
  blocking Verifier findings are in one document, `VERSIONS.md`, both are self-descriptive prose
  about the bundle's own guards, and **both describe the bundle as worse than it is** (the
  provenance guard "never covered `FIGURES.md`" — it has since 0.3.2, 0 unmatched of 255; 17
  errata "unchecked" — each reasoned, none unaccounted). Each contradicts its own version table
  three lines above. **The no-typed-figure rule was extended to figures, to prose, and to
  `FIGURES.md`, and never to claims about what the guards do.** Six gauntlets have died in that
  gap. The practice's failure mode has never once been flattery.
- **THE MEASUREMENT HELD, UNDER THE HARDEST PASS THIS ARC HAS HAD.** Two roles independently
  recomputed and found **no numeric error**: 30 of 30 bundle hashes; the 13 upstream run and
  sidecar files the bundle cites but does not ship, checked against the parent repository; every
  Fisher exact test pooled and per-stratum; both Wilson bounds; every age-band cell; the
  confirmation-record counts; 53 published errata with **0 live regressions**. The adversary: *"the
  first pass on this bundle, in this arc, that found the arithmetic itself sound wherever it was
  tested."*
- **THE ONE GENUINELY NEW OBJECTION IS SCIENTIFIC, NOT PROSE, AND IT IS CONCEDED AND QUANTIFIED.**
  The citation panel's construction date is recorded **nowhere**: 47 corpus files examined, exactly
  **1** carries any timestamp, and that one is the newest citation in the pool, not the pull. The
  record can only bracket it — at or after 2026-08-01T22:33:14Z, before 2026-08-11T11:24:06Z, a
  **9.5353-day window** (`panel-date-125.json`). The asymmetry is the finding: this arc dates
  `t_ref_utc` to the second and caught a 2.6803-day drift in it, while the clock behind the
  population every age-band figure rests on was never written down. It leaves a **survivorship
  confound** open — editions prune dead citations at rates that are themselves a function of
  citation age, the same axis the table is built on — so the gradient is what an undated snapshot
  showed, not a general yardstick. Closing it needs a dated methods statement, not a re-measurement.
- **THE ADVERSARY FOUND A RESULT IN OUR OWN DATA THAT WE HAD NOT LOOKED FOR.** Of the non-control
  panel (3,620 identifiers), only **7** ever show more than one determinate state across six
  independent days, and **412 of the 446 ever-absent identifiers are absent on every day they were
  measured (92 %)**. The bundle leans on a 9-event transition-confirmation sample while sitting on
  far stronger indirect confirmation in `series/presence-series.csv`. It was there for six days.
- **The window reached its pre-registered length.** Day 7 (2026-08-17T03:41:00Z) is the **seventh
  consecutive daily run** of the window pre-registered as *"seven consecutive daily runs (through
  2026-08-18)"*. The attached kill condition — zero transitions across seven runs kills the
  daily-series design — **does not fire**: transitions were recorded and confirmed. The design
  survives its own pre-registered test.
- **The closing question was asked and answered, not deferred** (`CONDITIONS-125.md`): **neither**
  keep polishing nor retire the measurement — six failures are six instances of one small,
  mechanisable defect class, and the measurement is sound. But the adversary's operational charge
  is accepted in full: *"there is, as of today, no remaining technical reason for this bundle to
  still be sitting on disk instead of in an inbox — which makes the fact that it still is the most
  damning finding in this report… It is a finding about the practice."* **The deliverable is the
  sentence, not the apparatus.** The next session repairs stale sentences as edits, closes the
  panel-date limit, adds the 92 % result, extends the no-typed-figure rule to claims about the
  guards, and runs **one** gauntlet — with a **hard stop**: if it fails, the bundle is retired as
  the delivery object and the arc ships the instrument.
